state is pruned), its notices show there, and `FormDoc.scripts` follows.
Nothing is reopened.

**The `--selftest-*` and `--script` harnesses** keep working through
`commit`. Their `dump` gains `"version"`.

#### 14.2 Open, Session and the CLI

**`sheaf_doc::open::start(bytes, password, store) -> Loading`** gives
the page count and the first page's geometry in O(1), and rendering
starts immediately.

**`open::finish(loading, progress) -> Opened { doc: Document }`** runs
on a job thread and builds the complete root:
- geometry, annotations and the form;
- the outline with styles and closed state, and labels;
- generators from markers;
- the synthesized `SaveRecord`.

Until it finishes:
- the app grays out edit and plugin commands;
- `commit` returns `NotReady`;
- plugin calls are queued until ready, and plugins get `opened` only
  after.

If a file misses the 1 s budget, edits simply wait longer, with progress
shown. There are no late facts (proposal 28).

**One open for the app and headless.** `sheaf_ops::open` (open.rs) is
replaced.

```rust
pub struct Session { pub doc: Document }                       // sheaf-ops: no GPUI; EnginePool only when rendering
impl Session {
    pub fn open(path: &Path, pw: Option<&str>, p: &Progress) -> Result<Session>;   // returns after finish
    pub fn new_empty() -> Session;                              // scan, images-to-pdf, summary
    pub fn intern(&mut self, path: &Path, pw: Option<&str>) -> Result<Blob>;       // pinned by the Session
    pub fn name(&mut self, name: &str, blob: Blob);             // for BlobSel::With in JSON
    pub fn commit(&mut self, ops: Vec<Op>, label: &str) -> Result<Commit, EditError>;  // actor = Cli, base = cursor
    pub fn preview(&self, ops: &[Op]) -> Result<Preview, EditError>;
    pub fn save(&mut self, target: SaveTarget, answers: &AskAnswers, p: &Progress) -> Result<SaveReport, SaveStop>;
    pub fn export(&self, e: Export, answers: &AskAnswers, p: &Progress) -> Result<ExportReport, ExportError>;
}
```

**Combine** is `Session::open(first)` plus one `Tx` (proposal 35):
- `DeletePages` for the first file's unselected pages;
- one `InsertPages { pages }` per further file, with its file name;
- `AddBookmark` per file.

The first file stays the primary, so its catalog keys (`/Lang`,
`/OCProperties`, structure, `/ViewerPreferences`, `/PageMode`,
`/Metadata`, `/OutputIntents`, `/Names`, and the AcroForm's `/XFA`,
`/DR`, `/DA` and `/Perms`) flow through `keep_only` and the form rules
as today. Its encryption is kept by `Profile.encryption = Keep`, subject
to proposal 10. The CLI then saves to `-o`.

**`sheaf edit IN --edits FILE|-`.** Flags:

| Flag | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `--with NAME=PATH[:PASSWORD_ENV]`… | name inputs for `{"with": "NAME"}` |
| `--password PW` | the password for IN |
| `-o OUT [--append]` or `--in-place` | the target; `--append` copies the saved bytes and appends, which keeps signatures |
| `--signatures ask\|break\|append-anyway` | how to answer a signature ask |
| `--xfa ask\|drop` | how to answer `XfaHoldsValues` |
| `--dry-run` | print the preview's lines and consequences and the resolved pages |
| `--force` | **only** "replace an existing output", as in every other subcommand |
| `--schema` | print the schemars schema |

- **The input** is the envelope (§6.1), whose `label` names the
  version.
- **JSON addressing** has no session ids: pages by `{"pos": n}` (pre-op
  state, 1-based), bookmarks by `{"path": [...]}`, annotations by
  `{"nm": …}`, blobs by `{"with": "NAME"}`. M10 adds fields by full
  name. An `Id` ref (a bare number) is `Invalid`: "session ids aren't
  meaningful in a file".
- **Output.** It prints `{version, created, lines, strategy}`, where
  `created` gives positions, `/NM` names and title paths, never session
  ids.
- **Exit codes:**
  - 0, done;
  - 1, failed;
  - 2, usage;
  - **3, a decision is needed** (the `Ask` is printed as JSON on
    stdout);
  - 130, canceled.

**`sheaf inspect IN [--password PW] --json`** prints, for scripts and
M15's authors:
- pages: position, label, boxes, rotate;
- sheaf marks: `/NM`, page, kind;
- others' comments: `/NM`, page;
- bookmarks as title paths;
- generators: kind, scope;
- a form summary.

**Every existing subcommand becomes a thin translator over `Session`:**
combine, comments import (`AddMark`/`PatchMarks` with `AnnotRef::Nm`
and `imported`, §6.8), remove-blanks, scan, images-to-pdf, split, export
and form check.

#### 14.3 Plugins: `sheaf:plugin@0.4.0`, one world

```wit
package sheaf:plugin@0.4.0;
interface edits {
  /// `ops`: the JSON envelope {"v":"1.N","ops":[…]} of sheaf-edit Ops (schema in docs/plugins.md, by schemars).
  /// Lowered on the plugin thread into this call's transaction; returns JSON `Applied { created, lines }`
  /// (ids final once the call commits) or JSON `EditError`.
  submit: func(label: string, ops: string) -> result<string, string>;
  preview: func(ops: string) -> result<string, string>;           // lines, effects in words, caps
  /// Commit this call's transaction now (blocks while the UI thread commits); the version, or a conflict.
  commit: func() -> result<version, string>;
}
interface assets {
  /// Bytes the plugin supplies (a PNG for a stamp, a file to attach): a handle valid for this call only.
  upload: func(bytes: list<u8>, kind: asset-kind) -> result<asset, string>;
}
interface document {
  record version { instance: u64, id: u64 }
  version: func() -> version;                                     // the call's base
  pages: func() -> list<page-info>;          // id, media, crop (PageSpace), rotate (absolute), label, to-view, from-view
  annotations: func(page: page-id) -> list<annot-view>;   // sheaf marks, plus others' comments as read-only summaries;
                                                          // never widgets; author as a role unless `identity`
  …                                                       // outline, selection, text (no field text), render
}
// events: opened (when ready), changed(change-info { version, parent, cause, actor-kind, scopes }), saved, edit-refused
// scopes: { order: bool, geometry: list<page-id>, content: list<page-id>, annots: list<page-id>,
//           outline: bool, labels: bool, form: bool }     — semantic, mapped from ChangeSet; Key stays internal
```

**Ops cross WIT as JSON of the exact Rust type** (proposal 18).
- sheaf-plugin-api re-exports `sheaf_edit::Op`, an `Ops` builder, and
  the `to_view`/`from_view` helpers. There is no hand mirror.
- **Versioning.** The manifest declares `edit-schema = "1.N"`. The host
  refuses a plugin whose minor is newer than its own at load, and the
  submit envelope carries the same version.
- Growth is additive: a new `Op` needs no WIT bump. Only new *read*
  functions bump the minor.

**One transaction per call.**
- **The base.** The host builds the call's `Tx` lazily, at the call's
  first document read or submit, from the document's `ArcSwap` cell. A
  queued call, or a command that first shows a form, therefore starts
  from a fresh base.
- **Reads.** Every WIT read records **decision reads** in the call's Tx:
  - `pages()` → `Order` and each listed page's `Page` and `Geom`;
  - `annotations(p)` → `PageAnnots(p)` and each `Annot`;
  - outline reads → `OutlineRoots` and the bookmarks visited;
  - `text`/`render(p)` → `read_render`.

  Reads see the plugin's own writes.
- **`submit`** lowers on the plugin thread (it needs only
  `Arc<LowerCx>`), returns the allocated ids and validates
  synchronously. Raw blob ids are refused; assets come through `upload`.
- **Committing.** `commit()` (optional) and the end-of-call `Ok` commit
  on the UI thread with `Stale::Relower`:
  - the decision reads are validated by value;
  - the ops are re-lowered on the cursor, with pinned ids;
  - if the re-lowered `created` differs from what `submit` returned, it
    is a Conflict.

  So a user edit to an unrelated page during a call doesn't refuse the
  call, while a change to anything the plugin read does.
- **After a mid-call `commit()`,** the call's next Tx is based on the
  version it committed. The version's label is the first submit's label.
- **On `Err`, a trap or cancel,** the uncommitted Tx is dropped, and
  `rollback_group` truncates or reverts (§8.4).
- **A conflict** surfaces as `commit`'s error or, at the end of the
  call, as a notice plus an `edit-refused` event.
- **One undo step.** A call's commits form one undo step, unless the
  user edits in between (§8.4, proposal 19).

**Reads are "as the file will draw it now"** for content: render and
text come from realizations with `MarkDraw::ByEngine`, unsaved header
and OCR included, with a `without-markup` option. Coordinates are
PageSpace, and the view matrices are provided.

**Privacy in 0.4:**
- `annotations()` never returns widgets.
- Text excludes field display text (`TextOpts { fields: false }`).
- Authors are roles, and identity tokens stay literal (§6.8).
- Renders draw appearances, as they do today. Gating field values in
  renders (`FormDraw::Redacted`) arrives with 0.5's fields capability
  (M10 slice 29).

**Events.**
- **`changed`** is coalesced per plugin by **merging**: the earliest
  `parent`, the latest `version`, the union of scopes, and cause
  `coalesced` when causes differ. A plugin can trust
  `parent == my last version`, or else re-read.
- **No echoes.** A plugin isn't sent its own changes. A causal depth
  stops edits made from `on_event` at depth ≥ 2.
- **No undo fights.** Edits submitted from an `on_event` whose
  triggering change was an Undo, Redo, Jump, Rollback or Revert are
  refused with an `edit-refused` event ("the user undid this"), so an
  event plugin can't fight undo.

**First cut:** `Cap::Plugin` ops. These are today's 13 WIT 0.3 edit
cases (wit/sheaf.wit:242–256: move, rotate, delete and duplicate pages;
the five outline edits; annot add, update and delete; connector add),
plus labels, replies and review states through locals, bookmark style,
page stamps and attachments through uploaded assets, and deleting
**sheaf marks**.
Header/footer, OCR, insert-pages and deleting others' comments come
later, with manifest permissions.

**One world.**
- **Deleted:** `wit/0.2`, `api/v02.rs`, `api/v03.rs` (0.4's host code
  replaces it), the double bindgen and `include!(shared.rs)`, the
  `World` enum and per-plugin world selection, `API_PREVIOUS`, the
  164,420-byte 0.2 fixture wasm,
  `keep_unseen`, `sign_marks`, `edit_source`'s batch loop, and
  `EditBatch`/`CallOutput.batches`.
- The manifest check is exact-minor until alpha.

**The bundled plugins are rebuilt, and their versions bumped:**
- tax-form-bookmarks appends instead of folding in reverse;
- bookmark-from-pattern uses locals;
- amount-crossref and duplicate-pages key `PENDING` by
  `version.instance`;
- subtotal invalidates by `scopes.annots`.

#### 14.4 The agent

Nothing is built for the agent in M10.5. The API leaves room for it to
expand later without reshaping:
- `Actor` is `#[non_exhaustive]`;
- version tokens are `{ instance, version }`;
- commits validate decision reads by value;
- `revert_group` exists;
- Op JSON is versioned.

§20 records the rules for its milestone.

### 15. Private data after M10.5

`sheaf_doc::private::KEYS` is enumerable. Sanitize and
`RemovePrivateData` read it. Everything else is standard keys.

| Key | Where | How Strip removes it |
|---|---|---|
| `/NM (sheaf:<id>)` | sheaf marks | rewritten to a neutral `/NM` |
| `/SheafAnnot` v2 | mark annotation dicts | dict key removed |
| `/SheafGenerators` | catalog JSON: specs only, no paths | catalog key removed |
| `/SheafBlock` | stream dict of each sheaf block and isolation stream | stream-dict key removed |
| `/SheafGen` | named `/Artifact` property list in `/Resources /Properties` | property-dict key removed |
| `/SheafOcrBlock` | named `/ShOCR` property list | property-dict key removed |
| legacy, read only: `/SheafStamp`, `SheafSettings`, `/SheafHF`, `/SheafHeaderFooter`, `/SheafPart`, `/SheafOcrLayer`, `/SheafTick*`; the resource names `/SheafOcr`, `/SheafFlat1`, `/SheafWM*` | inline property lists, stream dicts, `/Name`, resource dicts | `Filter::StripLegacy`, a lexical operand rewrite in the splice machinery (property-list keys dropped; resource names renamed to `/Sh…` in the page's `PageRes`), or a dict edit |

- A test fails if any other `Sheaf*` name appears in written document
  output.
- Stamp-library collection files (`/SheafStampText`, stamp_lib.rs) are
  the user's own library files. They are outside the edit API and
  outside this scan, and keep their format (proposal 29).
- Page-stamp appearance XObjects are named `PageRes`-style (`/Sh…`).
  `/SheafStamp` is read only.

### 16. Every feature on the new API

| # | Feature | New expression | Slice |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Move / reorder | `MovePages` → `Put Order`; Append rewrites `/Pages` nodes in place (+ labels by `emit_deps`) | 10, 11 |
| 2 | Rotate | absolute `Geom.rotate`; the render job carries the delta | 5 |
| 3 | Delete pages | `DeletePages` + rules page.annots, page.bookmarks, page.labels, page.generators, widget.field | 11 |
| 4 | Insert file | `InsertPages { pages, name }`, one form instance, notices, `Relower` | 11, 12 |
| 5 | Duplicate | `DuplicatePages` (copy + 1, private annotations, new form instance) | 11 |
| 6 | Replace | `ReplacePages` (same ids, base swap) | 11 |
| 7 | Crop / Trim White | `SetPageBoxes`; Trim White = content-only render job, `Validate` | 15 |
| 8 | Watermark / background | `SetWatermark` / `RemoveWatermark` generators; `ImageRef`; `replaces` | 16 |
| 9 | Header/footer + Bates | `SetHeaderFooter` / `RemoveHeaderFooter`; live tokens; frozen Bates per block | 17 |
| 10 | Strip ours and Acrobat's | filters by origin; `PageRes` pruning; authored only on request | 16, 17 |
| 11 | OCR layers | `SetOcrText` / `ClearOcrText`; blocks; content-only input; OCR group | 8, 18 |
| 12 | Sheaf marks | `AddMark` / `PatchMarks` / `MoveMarks` / `DeleteAnnotations`; v2 digest; stable objects | 5, 6, 10, 11 |
| 13 | Page / custom stamps | `Mark.stamp = FormRef::Blob`; `place_form`; library stamps as `Memory(Library)` | 6, 19 |
| 14 | Tapes | marks with derived AP; turned pictures in memory | 6 |
| 15 | Connectors | `AddConnector`; `/Dest` resolved symbolically at emit | 9, 11 |
| 16 | Measurements | unchanged kinds, in PageSpace | 5 |
| 17 | Attachments (marks) | `Mark.file = BlobRef`, embedded once per file | 6, 8 |
| 18 | Others' comments | `AnnotBody::Foreign`; `DeleteAnnotations`; `AdoptAnnotations` (keeps source keys, drops `/RC` on text change); hiding is a view input | 8, 11, 13 |
| 19 | Outline | per-item `Bookmark` records; emitted in place by provenance | 8, 9 |
| 20 | Page labels | `SetPageLabels`; anchors validated; re-anchor cascade | 9, 11 |
| 21 | Fields / widgets | `FieldNode` tree with `FormInst`, widgets as annotations (merged flag, props), `FormDoc`; M10 slices 4–5's rules as lowering + `rules::plan` | 8, 9, 10 |
| 22 | Blank pages | `InsertPages { from: Blank }` → `Memory(Blank)` | 19 |
| 23 | Images → PDF, scan | `InsertPages { from: Images }`; scan = Session + `SetOcrText` + save | 19 |
| 24 | Combine | open first + one Tx; the app hands the Document to a new window | 11, 19 |
| 25 | Extract / split | `KeepPages` presets | 19 |
| 26 | Flatten (export, print with markup) | `FlattenAnnotations` with `PinnedAp` | 19 |
| 27 | Clean copy, print without markup | `RemoveMarkup` + `RemovePrivateData` (with `StripLegacy`) | 19 |
| 28 | Print, page images/text, print preview | ExportTx → bytes → pool; preview realizes the print state | 14, 19 |
| 29 | Layers (OCG) | `ViewInputs.ocg_off` | 13 |
| 30 | Comments import (XFDF) | `AddMark` / `PatchMarks` with `AnnotRef::Nm` and `imported` (`/NM`, dates, author kept), `Relower` | 12, 19 |
| 31 | Remove blanks, comment summary | export presets; summary on ContentWriter as a `Memory(Summary)` source | 6, 19 |
| 32 | Recovery | Full (slice 9), then Append sections with form transforms and a removals flag, encrypted, chained | 9, 10 |
| 33 | Save in place | planner from effects; SaveRecord; in-place append; no rebase | 9, 10 |
| 34 | Stamp library | stays outside the edit API (user files); placing a stamp interns its form | 6 |
| 35 | Set Status, Color by Level, Bookmarks from Pages, paste, calculator | UI macros emitting op batches with locals through `Tx::reader` | 11 |
| 36 | Replace mark (Caret + StrikeOut) | companion recognized by `/IRT` + `/RT /Group` to a sheaf Caret | 9 |
| 37 | Plugins | WIT 0.4 JSON ops, assets, decision reads | 20 |
| 38 | Save As | a Save; copy-and-append for signed documents | 9, 10 |

- written no more: `/SheafTick<id>` and `/SheafAnnot` v1 (5);
  `/SheafStamp` as a stamp XObject name (6); `/SheafPart` and recovery
  sidecar v1 (9); `SheafSettings`, `/SheafWM*` and `/SheafStamp` as a
  watermark's artifact property (16); `/SheafHeaderFooter`, `/SheafHF`
  and `/SheafStamp` as a header's artifact property (17);
  `/SheafOcrLayer` and `/SheafOcr` as the OCR font's resource name
  (18); `/SheafFlat1` (19);
- the legacy ones are read for recognition, and `RemovePrivateData`
  strips them.

**Enforcement.** `tests/retired.rs` (from slice 1) lists every retired
identifier as a qualified path, such as `save::subset`,
`text_layer::search` or `incremental::Mode`, with its slice, the same
one as above. It matches with a `syn` walk of `crates/`, excluding
`vendor/` and the identifiers the new design reuses: `Mode`, `Step`,
`PageRef`, `LabelSpec` and `Widget` are matched only qualified. Each
slice adds its own rows in the same commit.

### 18. Invariants changed deliberately

- **Coordinates and identity:**
  - Rotation is absolute.
  - Coordinates are PageSpace everywhere, including `/SheafAnnot` v2, Op
    JSON and the plugin API, which also gets view helpers.
  - A VersionId names one immutable state, and an amend replaces the
    version.
  - Commits validate what they read and overwrite, by value.
- **Saves:**
  - Text layers are blocks. Presence no longer means unsaved, and a
    saved layer can be removed.
  - A save re-expresses nothing. Dirty is `saved != cursor`, and the
    version being saved is sealed.
  - A file changed on disk is never overwritten without asking.
  - Save As of a signed document copies the file and appends.
  - Removal detection is strict and content-based, and a regenerated
    page number is the one exemption. On a signed document, a removal
    veto asks.
- **Annotations and fields:**
  - Others' comments are records, and `removed_comments` is retired.
  - A field whose last modeled widget is deleted is removed.
  - A stale `/SheafAnnot` opens as another tool's comment, and a
    duplicated `sheaf:` name gets a fresh id.
  - Copied widgets and annotations are untagged. Copies, and pages from
    other files' structure trees, lose `/StructParents`.
- **Generated content:**
  - A label range whose start page is deleted re-anchors to the next
    page of the range.
  - Header/footer page tokens are live in every output. Bates numbers
    and date and file tokens are frozen at apply and persisted per
    block. Watermarks stay upright.
  - Ours replaces Acrobat-added headers, footers and watermarks of the
    same kind. Authored pagination artifacts are never touched without
    `include_authored`.
  - Unencodable glyphs draw `'?'` with a notice, in marks and
    generators, until M10 slice 7.
- **Callers:**
  - Plugin calls are one undo step unless the user edits in between.
  - Combine opens its result in a new window without a reopen.

### 19. New public types: what they replace, what produces them

| Types | Replace | Produced by in M10.5 |
|---|---|---|
| `Store`, `Blob`, `BlobRef`, `Src`, `BlobId` | `SourceInfo`, `add_source`, the generated cache | open, insert, intern, upload |
| `Source`, `LazyPdf`, `XrefFacts`, facts | eager `load_source` per use, six late loaders | open, insert |
| `DocState`, `Key`, `Val`, `Prim` | DocState v1, `Edit`'s implicit effects | every op |
| `PageDef`, `PageBase`, `Geom` | today's `model::PageRef` struct, `Rebase`, `PageGeom`, `geom_of` | open, page ops |
| `ContentStack`, `Layer`, `Static`, `Filter`, `MarkedSel`, `StreamSel`, `LegacySel`, `PagOrigin`, `Placement`, `FormRef`, `PinnedAp` | derived copies, `text_layers`, `stamps::strip` | OCR, flatten, remove/replace, `RemovePrivateData` |
| `Generator`, `GenSpec`, `Scope`, `Frozen`, `ImageRef`, `PageForm`, `CtxNeeds` | `stamps::HeaderFooter`, derive::watermark | Set/Remove Watermark and HeaderFooter |
| `AnnotRec`, `Mark`, `Foreign`, `Widget`, `WidgetProps` | per-page `Annot` lists, `foreign_annots`, `removed_comments`, `forms::Widget`'s ObjectId/slot | open, mark ops |
| `FieldNode`, `Terminal`, `FormDoc`, `FormInst`, `ScriptState` | `Field { copy, under, object }`, `Document.forms`, `scripts_mixed` | open, insert, duplicate |
| `Bookmark`, `Target`, `LabelSpec` | `OutlineNode`, today's `LabelSpec` | open, bookmark and label ops |
| `Op`, `Transform`, `PageRef` (the op reference enum), `BookmarkRef`, `AnnotRef`, `BlobSel`, `PageSel`, `Patch`, `NewMark`, `MarkPatch`, `Imported`, `Bring`, `Delta` | `Edit`, the WIT edit variant, plugin-api `Batch` | app, CLI, plugins |
| `Tx`, `ExportTx`, `LowerCx`, `HostAttrs`, `Notice`, `EditError`, `Created`, `Preview` | `compute`, `EditOutcome`, `sign_marks` | every caller |
| cascade rules | `cascade_delete`, `import_marks`, `merge_outline` | lowering |
| `Effects`, `Flags`, `SigClass`, `classify`, `emit_deps` | `removes_content`, dirty flags, `saved_*` | commits, saves |
| `Version`, `Step`, `Line`, `GroupId`, `GroupKind`, `ChangeSet`, `Commit`, `Base`, `Stale` | in-place versions, jobs, three funnels | every transition |
| `SaveRecord`, `SaveInput`, `SavePlan`, `Strategy`, `Onto`, `Why`, `Ask`, `AppendAnyway`, `SigImpact`, `FileStamp`, `ContentHash`, `PendingTail`, `SaveTransform` | `Signed`, `WouldBreakSignatures`, `rebase_to_saved`, `rules::take_out` | every save |
| `ObjKey`, `SymObject`, `Emission`, `EmitScope`, `ObjMap` | `build_rewrite`/incremental internals | materialize, realize |
| `Export`, `Profile`, `Out`, presets | `export_*`, `print_pdf`, `subset` | exports |
| `ViewInputs`, `RenderKey`, `EngineKey`, `PageView`, `Realizer`, `EnginePool` | `RenderHandle` per source, `hide_annots`, `CharsKey` | screen, print, OCR, plugins |
| `TextService`, `TextOpts`, `PageChars` | `layer_hits`, `merge_source_hits` | Find, plugins, blank detection |
| `Tok`, `Marked`, `Balance`, `PageBlocks`, `TextBag`, `ContentWriter`, `Font` | `Content::decode/encode` use, four encoders | content features, removal check |
| `Session` | `sheaf_ops::open`, per-command flows | CLI, combine, scan |

### 20. What later milestones build on, and the rules recorded for them

**M11 (redaction, sanitize, security):**
- **Slice 1 shrinks** to notices, Properties, owner-only files, lopdf #584,
  the certificate refusal and temp cleanup. Encryption
  preservation already landed in slices 7, 9 and 10 here.
- **`SetSecurity`:**
  - `Key::Security` holds the spec with secret handles: `Zeroizing`,
    redacted in `Debug`, and excluded from `Step.op` JSON, describe
    lines and serde.
  - Binder saves encrypt with the document's security (`FromDocument`).
    `Profile.encryption = New(spec)` is for exports only.
  - `EncryptionChanged` forces a Rewrite, and recovery is written in
    Full form, encrypted with the target security.
- **The interpreter** builds on `content/` (lex, splice, `Balance`,
  TextBag).
  - `ContentStack.rewrite` is "the new base": setting it absorbs the
    current filters (cleared), and later filters apply to its output.
  - `Static::Ops` and redaction output are **definitions**
    (`Rewrite::Redact { areas, policy }`, `Rewrite::Edit { ops }`),
    evaluated per page at emit with a bounded digest cache, as
    `SymObject::Deferred` bodies.
  - `ApplyRedactions` and `Sanitize` take a `PageSel`, so Preview lowers
    only visible pages. Lowering reports progress per page.
  - Partial-image redaction decodes through PDFium in the host resolve
    stage (§6.3).
- **The leak check** runs over the **output**: a walk of the Emission
  (Built values plus Raw source objects reachable from the roots), with
  content and appearance strings decoded, plus the text service over the
  materialized bytes. It covers Info and XMP, embedded-file names, named
  destinations, link URIs and Launch targets, structure `/Alt` and
  `/ActualText`, `/TU`, JavaScript, `/PieceInfo` and `/SheafAnnot`. A
  DocState pass (`modeled_text`) is only a fast pre-check.
- **Plugins never apply redactions,** because `ApplyRedactions` is a
  `Transform`. If apply-in-place arrives, it becomes an `Op` with
  `Cap::User`.
- **Plugin API:** M10 takes 0.5 and M11 takes 0.6.

**M12 (signatures):**
- **Signing is two ordinary commits around one Append:**
  1. `SignField { field, appearance, reason, mdp: Option<DocMdp>, lock: Option<FieldLock> }`
     lowers to Puts, so locks and DocMDP are model data that lowering
     enforces.
  2. The save runs with `Profile.reserve` (a zero-filled `/Contents`
     hole and a fixed-width `/ByteRange`). `serialize/` returns the
     hole's offsets.
  3. After the CMS is injected,
     `SetSignatureValue { field, contents: BlobRef, byte_range }`
     commits, and `record_save` attaches the record to that version, so
     `emit(state)` reproduces the file byte for byte.
- **Signing requires `plan == Append`.** Otherwise the user saves first,
  through its asks, then signs.
- **`SaveRecord.signatures`.** `SigImpact`, lock checks, the signature
  panel and revisions use `Source::signatures()` ∪ the saved chain's
  signatures.
- **`SigClass` gains SIGN and LTV.** DSS and document timestamps
  classify as LTV, which PAdES allows after P1, and `Key::Dss` is added.
- **"Changes since signing"** uses a content-based role classifier over
  the objects of later revisions (`/Type /Annot`, `/Subtype /Widget`,
  `/FT`, `/Sig`, `/Page`, catalog keys). The `ObjKey` mapping is only a
  test oracle for sheaf's own Appends.
- **Already in place from M10.5:** Save As copy-and-append,
  `Source::revisions()` and the Append serializer.

**M13 (object editing):**
- `AnnotBody::Link { rect, quads, target: Target, border, h, src, copy }`.
- `KeyPatch` role rows and `PatchValue` targets for link `/Dest` and
  `/A` edits (the type itself is M10 slice 10's).
- `Static::Image { blob, matrix }`.
- An object query that unifies base items and placed layers, and a
  content hide set in `ViewInputs`.
- `Source::users` and the in-model users index arrive with the first
  "all uses" op.

**M14 (optimize, PDF/A, accessibility):**
- **Object streams.** Packing in Rewrite and Append, with
  `packable(ObjKey, &SymObject, gen)` encoding ISO 32000-1 7.5.7's
  exclusions: streams, generation ≠ 0, `/Encrypt`, an ObjStm's own
  `/Length`, xref streams, and signature holders. Member strings are
  never encrypted individually; the container is.
- **Fonts.**
  - `Profile.fonts: Std14Ok | EmbedAll` is part of the `appearance()`
    and `layout()` digests and of `Res::Font` resolution.
  - Metric-compatible substitutes (Liberation) sit behind `Font`.
  - Foreign non-embedded fonts need an all-uses patch.
- **PDF/A claims.** Never write transparency into a PDF/A-1 source, and
  offer to remove a claim explicitly. M10.5 only adds the Note.
- **Structure.** `Key::StructElem`, `/ParentTree` emitted from the
  model, and `PlaceTag::Struct(elem)` for flatten. These replace M10.5's
  "drop `/StructParents`" rule.
- **Save Optimized** writes an export to the path, then reopens it as a
  new session, with an explicit "this clears undo history" notice.
  Alternatively, only Save As Optimized is offered.

**M15 (batch):**
- **A versioned step schema** above `Op`:
  `Step = Op(Op) | Job(JobSpec { Ocr, TrimWhite, RedactPattern, … }) | Export(Preset, Vec<Transform>)`.
  A host batch runner executes it as `Actor::Batch` with the user's
  caps.
- **`BatesStart::Continue`**, resolved by the runner, and
  `PageSel::{Label, Matches}`.

**The agent milestone:**
- **Transport and entry points:**
  - its own transport and approval UI;
  - `Actor::Agent`;
  - `Document::plan(actor, base, ops) -> PlanHandle` (a lowered `Tx`
    plus a `Preview`), and `commit(plan)`, so the approval card commits
    exactly what it showed;
  - plans expire on conflict.
- **Reads.**
  - The agent's reads are decision reads, kept per MCP session, and it
    commits `Validate`, so it can write during OCR.
  - Field values sit behind [`agent.md`](agent.md)'s per-document
    consent.
- **"Revert this turn"** is a revert of the turn's versions. A jump is
  offered only when nothing came after the turn.

## Proposed (to confirm at kickoff)

Greg's standing instruction is to take the recommended answer unless he
objects, so each of these stands as written until he does. Proposals
8–10 are open questions for him, marked below and listed first under
Asks.

8. (Open for Greg.) **Header/footer page numbers are live; Bates numbers
   and the date and file tokens are frozen at apply.**
   - `<<1>>`, `<<N>>` and `<<label>>` are re-evaluated in every output:
     the binder after a reorder, an extract, a print. The page says what
     it is, and exports and prints match.
   - The cost: a reorder after a save re-lays out every covered page. On
     a 7,000-page document with "Page n of N", the next Append writes up
     to 7,000 small block streams and 7,000 page dicts (a few MB), and
     the realizer re-renders those pages. That cost is bounded and
     measured (slice 17's live-token reorder gate: at most 8 MB and 1 s
     for the Append).
   - The machinery it needs: `CtxNeeds` positions in render keys,
     `LIVE_REDRAW`, `SaveRecord.drawn`, and allowed drops in the removal
     check.
   - The alternative is frozen numbers plus an "Update page numbers"
     action, as in Acrobat. A reorder then writes one `Order` value and
     appends only `/Pages` nodes, and the machinery above goes; but the
     numbers are stale until the user updates them, and extracts keep
     the binder's numbers.
   - Bates numbers and the date and file tokens are frozen either way.
   - If that gate fails on the heavy fixture, fall back to frozen
     numbers plus Update. Slice 17's notes record the result as this
     proposal's verdict.
9. (Open for Greg.) **Replacing text that is already in the saved file
   forces a full rewrite** (strict removal).
   - It covers mark text, a bookmark title, a label prefix, a field
     value, OCR words, header or watermark text, and anything else
     already in the saved file. Today only removals force one.
   - It extends M9's "deleting has to delete" and M10 proposal 7's "a
     replaced value forces a full rewrite" to every replaced text, and
     the removal check (§11.9) backs it up.
   - The cost: a note edit or a bookmark rename after a save becomes a
     Rewrite (about 0.5 s on the synthetic 7k, more on large scans), and
     signed files ask more often. "Append anyway" is offered on them,
     keeping the old text in the earlier revision.
   - A regenerated page number is the one exemption. Geometry and style
     changes append.
10. (Open for Greg.) **Exported copies keep the source's encryption by
    default.**
    - It covers Extract, Split, Export Flattened and Clean Copy. Today
      exports are unencrypted, because loads strip `/Encrypt`.
    - A password-protected client file shouldn't produce unprotected
      copies silently.
    - Print and page images stay unencrypted bytes in memory either way.
      Save As is a Save, so it always keeps the encryption.
    - No checkbox per dialog in M10.5. M11's security UI adds the
      choice.
11. **M10 resumes only after all of M10.5 (slices 1–20).**
    - It keeps Greg's sequencing (decision 2): M10's slices 6+ are built
      on the finished API, and no M10.5 slice trails.
    - Slice 20 rewrites `m10.md`'s slices 6+ to match (M10 slices 6+ on
      the new API, below).
    - M10's slices 6–9 shrink by about one slice: the spike, the
      re-binding and the per-writer save rules go.
12. **History stores resolved primitives with their before-values, and
    the snapshots stay.**
    - Replay applies the prims and checks each before-value. It never
      re-runs lowering, so it can't drift when an input nobody recorded
      changes (bug 6), and a mismatch is reported, never a silent no-op.
    - Before-values cost nothing (they are the parent's Arcs) and buy
      `classify` from a step alone, the commit-time value check, and
      describe text for pruned parents.
    - One primitive, `Put`, over 11 key kinds keeps the set closed.
      Ordered collections are values, not splices.
    - Prior art: ProseMirror's steps fail rather than repair; Slate's
      operations carry their before-values; Lexical keeps full
      snapshots, as sheaf does.
    - Snapshots stay, so undo and jump stay O(1); decision 3 holds the
      snapshot undo guarantees fixed.
13. **Versions are immutable, and commits validate by value, with two
    stale policies, `Validate` and `Relower`.**
    - An amend replaces the version with a new id, so a version token
      alone says whether anything changed (bug 7).
    - Validating by value is exact under amends, ignores changes that
      cancel out, and doesn't care whether the base was pruned.
    - `Relower` covers work whose ids nobody saw yet (inserts, blank
      review) and plugin calls with pinned ids, so a user edit to an
      unrelated page doesn't refuse them.
    - Three-way merges of ordered keys, and OT or CRDTs, stay out (Not
      in M10.5).
14. **Page content is a definition in the model: a content stack per
    page, and generators for the document.**
    - The derived copy exists only to change content, the M10 worker's
      "strongest argument for page content in the model". With content
      in the model, the page object, annotations, fields and structure
      parents stay where they are.
    - A stack holds definitions, not bytes. Additions never re-encode
      the original content, so an Append stays possible, and a
      regenerated result is a cache, never the definition (Onshape's
      lesson).
    - History, plugins and the planner can tell a crop (geometry) from a
      watermark (a generator) from a removal (a filter).
15. **One emitter feeds every output, and a save is an output, never a
    rebase.**
    - The full rewrite, the incremental append, the recovery section and
      the render overlay are four scopes of one `emit`, so the two
      writers can't drift (bug 14) and the screen equals the file.
    - The model never re-expresses a version after a save (bug 1). A
      `SaveRecord` carries what the next save needs.
    - Stable numbering keeps object identity across saves, so a changed
      mark is rewritten under the same number.
16. **The screen renders edited pages from in-memory append sections
    over the source's bytes.**
    - Measured at 7,000 pages: the original plus a 370-byte update
      section loads in 2.0–2.4 ms, and the two-segment
      `FPDF_LoadCustomDocument` reader avoids the copy (2.16 ms).
    - PDFium's `GenerateContent` is lossy, takes about 95 ms per change
      and mutates the live document. Per-page scratch PDFs, per-source
      generations and per-page overlays were considered and rejected
      (§10.1).
    - Slice 2's spikes decide the group size, the encrypted-base path
      and the box-edit path by numbers before anything depends on them.
17. **The content lexer, splicer, writer and fonts move forward from M11
    into `sheaf-doc/src/content/`.**
    - Removing headers and watermarks, recognizing blocks after another
      tool merged streams, pruning resources and the removal check all
      need them now.
    - M11's interpreter consumes `Tok` spans, so nothing is thrown away.
      The semantic interpreter stays in M11 (§13).
    - It retires lopdf's `Content` round trip (f32 numbers, broken
      inline images) and four text encoders.
18. **Plugin API 0.4 is the only world, and ops cross WIT as JSON of the
    Rust `Op`.**
    - 0.2 and 0.3 are no longer served (decision 3). The manifest check
      is exact-minor until alpha.
    - JSON of the exact type means no hand mirror, and a new `Op` needs
      no WIT bump; only new read functions bump the minor.
    - `deny_unknown_fields`, per-kind refs with clear errors, the
      published schema and the `edit-schema` manifest check make up for
      the compile-time typing non-Rust plugins lose.
    - M10's field reads become 0.5, and M11's API 0.6.
19. **A plugin call is one undo step. A trap, Err or cancel rolls it
    back by truncating or reverting.**
    - When the user edits in between, the call's versions are fragments
      of one group, undone one at a time or all together.
    - Today an Err, a cancel or a trap drops every batch silently, and a
      call that succeeds makes one version per batch.
20. **Plugins never learn the user's identity.**
    - Authors are roles, and `<<name>>` and `<<initials>>` stay literal
      without the `identity` manifest permission, which no bundled
      plugin asks for.
    - Today `sign_marks` fills them in with the user's name, which the
      plugin can read back (bug 27).
    - Field values in plugin renders are gated by 0.5's fields
      capability (M10 slice 29).
21. **Older sheaf marks (`/SheafAnnot` v1) are converted on read.**
    - Geometry comes from the standard keys, which are already
      PageSpace; the JSON supplies only what PDF can't hold.
    - It's nearly free, which is decision 3's bar for migration code.
22. **Unencodable text gets one interim rule for marks and generators:
    `'?'` in what's drawn, the exact text kept, and a notice, until M10
    slice 7.**
    - Emit stays total, so a later label change can't make a document
      unsaveable.
    - M10 slice 7's Noto fallback ends it, behind the same `Font` trait.
23. **Encryption preservation is built into M10.5's serializer and
    savers (slices 7, 9 and 10), and M11 slice 1 shrinks.**
    - The new serializer encrypts per object anyway, and recovery for
      encrypted files depends on it.
    - It ends M11 bug 2 and the "password protection removed" notice.
24. **Copied widgets and annotations are untagged until M14.** Pages
    copied, or inserted from other structure trees, lose
    `/StructParents`, with a Note when the source was tagged.
    - Keeping `/StructParents` on a copy would point into the original's
      parent tree. Modeling structure is M14's.
25. **Two cascade changes: a field whose last modeled widget is deleted
    is removed, and a label range whose first page is deleted re-anchors
    to the next page of its range.**
    - The first makes the model match what `keep_only` already writes.
      The model test `form_fields_follow_their_pages` (model.rs:3055;
      the assert at :3080) inverts.
    - The second keeps a range's numbering when its first page goes;
      today label anchors aren't validated at all.
26. **OCR text is untagged invisible text, with a private marker carried
    by a named property list.**
    - A named property list lets a merged stream still classify, and
      keeps Strip a dict edit.
27. **Crop is box-only.** Removing the content outside the box is M11's.
    - A crop then appends with only page dicts in the section.
28. **Edits are disabled until the open's facts are read.** There are no
    late facts: a slow file just waits longer.
    - Today six loaders drop facts that arrive after the first edit (bug
      10).
29. **The stamp library stays outside the edit API.**
    - Its files are the user's own library, not document content.
      Placing a stamp interns its form.
30. **Watermarks stay upright after a rotate, like headers.**
    - Layout happens in the displayed frame at emit, so a dialog's
      preview equals the result. Today a watermark's orientation is
      fixed at apply, and a header's at save.
31. **sheaf replaces Acrobat-added headers, footers and watermarks of
    the same kind automatically, and never removes authored ones (Word,
    InDesign) without `include_authored`. Dialogs show the counts.**
    - Acrobat's are recognized by their `/PieceInfo`
      `ADBE_CompoundType`. Anything else marked `/Artifact /Pagination`
      is an author's running head.
32. **Save As of a signed document copies the file and appends, so its
    signatures survive. Save As of an unsigned document rewrites.**
    - That is what Acrobat does.
33. **A file changed on disk always asks: Save As…, Overwrite or
    Cancel.**
    - Today a changed length silently falls through to a rewrite over
      the other program's changes (bug 2).
34. **UR3 is kept on Appends within its TransformParams, and dropped on
    every Rewrite.**
    - Reader validates later revisions against `/TransformParams`, so a
      permitted fill or comment keeps Reader's rights. A Rewrite always
      breaks UR3's signature.
35. **Combine opens the first file, makes one transaction, saves to the
    chosen path, and hands the document to the new window.**
    - Its notices and the scripts-off state then follow the document;
      today they can't reach the window Combine opens. The first file's
      catalog keys flow through as today.
36. **The agent socket is out of M10.5.**
    - Nothing in M10.5 produces or uses it. The agent milestone brings
      its own transport (§20).
37. **Object-stream packing moves to M14, and `keep_only` is not ported
    to a symbolic graph.**
    - Neither has an M10.5 producer. `keep_only` is ported only if slice
      9's Rewrite gate fails.
38. **M12's hooks are dropped from M10.5.** Reading revisions stays, for
    forms' EarlierValues.
39. **PDF/A and PDF/UA claims get a Note when a change breaks them.**
    Conversion and font embedding are M14's.
40. **The CLI:**
    - `--force` keeps meaning "overwrite" only, as in every other
      subcommand;
    - signature and XFA answers are explicit flags;
    - exit code 3 means a decision is needed, with the `Ask` as JSON, so
      a script never has a choice made for it silently;
    - `sheaf edit` addresses by position, title path, `/NM` and `--with`
      names;
    - `sheaf inspect --json` is added.

## Found while planning

Bugs and gaps in today's code that matter for M10.5. References are as
of `a0955fc`. "From the code" means read, not reproduced; "inferred"
means the survey reader concluded it without running it. The slice in
brackets fixes each.

1. **An edit made during a save is lost or misfiled.** From the code.
   - `DocView::save_to` snapshots the cursor and saves in the
     background, and edits stay enabled; only a second save is refused
     (docview.rs:1655).
   - When the save finishes, `rebase_to_saved` (model.rs:1208)
     re-expresses whatever version is the cursor *then* as the saved
     file: it clears that version's OCR layers, maps its pages by
     position onto the saved file's pages, and marks it saved.
   - So an OCR flush during the save loses its layers, a page move
     during the save shows the wrong saved pages in those positions, and
     any edit made during the save is marked saved without being in the
     file.

   [Slice 9.]
2. **A file changed on disk is overwritten without asking, and saves
   aren't durable.** From the code.
   - The only check is the file's length against what was opened
     (`SourceInfo.bytes`, save.rs:908). When it differs,
     `save_to_path_noting` falls through to a full rewrite and replaces
     the file (save.rs:923–946).
   - A change that keeps the length goes unnoticed, and the incremental
     save then appends to the bytes read at open (incremental.rs:65),
     not to the file on disk. Either way the other program's changes are
     discarded.
   - `write_atomic` (save.rs:954) uses one fixed temp name per file, so
     two saves collide, and it never fsyncs.

   [Slices 7 and 10.]
3. **The first copy in page order owns a page's object.**
   `build_rewrite` gives a page's original object to whichever copy
   comes first (`used.insert(src_page)`, save.rs:613). Move a duplicate
   before its original and save: the duplicate takes the original's
   object number, and the links, `/P` entries and structure that pointed
   at the original now point at the copy. From the code. [Slice 9.]
4. **Encryption is dropped on load, and lopdf's per-object crypto has
   gaps** (M11 bug 2).
   - `file::load_pdf_mem` and `load_pdf` remove `/Encrypt` (file.rs:258,
     :277), so every rewrite is plaintext, and the incremental writer
     refuses encrypted files (incremental.rs:73). The save notice says
     "password protection removed" (docview.rs:1749).
   - Vendored lopdf's `transform_object` (encryption.rs:734) exempts
     only xref streams and `/Metadata`, has no `/EFF` handling, and
     doesn't reach strings inside stream dicts (lopdf #603).

   [Slices 3 and 7.]
5. **An append to a file with bytes before `%PDF-` mixes offsets.**
   lopdf reads xref offsets relative to the first `%PDF-` (vendor
   reader.rs:686), and the incremental writer takes `/Prev` from it
   (incremental.rs:76, :495) but writes its new objects' offsets as
   absolute positions in the buffer (:484–546). It also copies `/ID`
   unchanged (:496–500). From the code. [Slice 7.]
6. **Replay isn't exact, and a failure is silent.**
   - `compute` (model.rs:2119) reads inputs no step records: the
     sources' page counts and sizes, the rng, `Document.forms` and path
     stems. `replay_step` (:1502) re-runs it.
   - `undo` and `redo` turn a replay failure into a log warning and do
     nothing (model.rs:1335, :1346).
   - The property test `replaying_the_log_rebuilds_every_version`
     (model.rs:3466) covers 7 of the 21 kinds.

   [Slice 1.]
7. **Versions change in place, and a no-op is found by deep
   comparison.**
   - `apply_in_job` (model.rs:1434–1463) amends the cursor version in
     place, guarded only by `saved != cursor`, and `rebase_to_saved`
     changes a version in place too. Whoever read version V can't tell
     that V changed.
   - No-op detection compares whole states (`state == *self.state()`,
     model.rs:1384, :1457). On stable Rust, im's `PartialEq` has no
     `ptr_eq` shortcut, so every apply compares every page, mark, OCR
     word, field and widget.

   [Slice 1.]
8. **History descriptions are computed against the wrong state.**
   `VersionView` describes every step of a batch or job against the
   parent state, so later steps print the wrong page numbers, and falls
   back to counts when the parent's snapshot was trimmed. describe.rs
   prints "Change the content of …" for every `RebasePages`
   (describe.rs:113), whatever the feature. [Slices 1 and 11.]
9. **History memory is guessed.** `marginal` (model.rs:1798–1814) counts
   256 bytes per changed annotation, widget or field and ignores step
   payloads. `HistoryStats.undone_source_bytes` is hard-coded to 0
   (model.rs:1715), and sources are never freed. Inferred: at 20,000
   fields a commit costs about 5 MB by this count, so the 256 MB budget
   lasts about 50 commits. [Slices 1, 3 and 12.]
10. **File facts that arrive late are dropped.** `opened_state_mut`
    (model.rs:760) works only before the first edit, so six loaders log
    "… arrived after edits; ignored" and drop their facts
    (docview.rs:1208, :1858, :1867, :1872, :1915; form_bar.rs:71).
    Plugins get `opened` before those loads finish, so a plugin can win
    the race (inferred). The app and headless opens are two
    implementations (`sheaf_ops::open`). [Slice 4.]
11. **Unknown ids are ignored, and references are never validated.**
    Move, Rotate, Delete, Duplicate and ClearTextLayers skip ids they
    don't know (their `compute` arms at model.rs:2122, :2153, :2170,
    :2248, :2618). Bookmark targets, label anchors and the
    header/footer's page scope are never checked. [Slice 11.]
12. **Cascades are written per edit, and differ.**
    - DeletePages (model.rs:2170) leaves the fields
      (`form_fields_follow_their_pages` asserts 9 remain,
      model.rs:3080), the label anchors and the header/footer's page
      scope.
    - DuplicatePages (model.rs:2248) doesn't copy `removed_comments`, so
      another tool's comment deleted on the original reappears on the
      copy. From the code.
    - ReplacePages keeps `removed_comments` on the extras it deletes.
    - RebasePages shifts marks and OCR words, but not widgets or
      bookmark views.

    [Slices 5, 8 and 11.]
13. **Removal is detected by id, so replaced text survives incremental
    saves.**
    - `removes_content` (model.rs:1156–1195) checks for missing primary
      pages, mark ids, deleted comments and bookmark ids, nothing else.
    - Replaced mark text, a renamed bookmark, a changed label prefix and
      a deleted mark from an inserted file all slip through (the last
      inferred), so the old text stays in the earlier revision. A
      replaced OCR layer is caught only because the incremental writer
      bails (incremental.rs:268, :322).
    - It counts any primary page shown from another source as removed,
      so a crop forces a full rewrite though it removes nothing (the M10
      worker's note).

    [Slice 10.]
14. **The incremental writer can't express enough, and writes too
    much.** `incremental::build` (incremental.rs:62):
    - parses the whole primary eagerly (:67);
    - can't draw a header/footer (:71), so `/SheafHeaderFooter` and the
      drawn Bates numbers go stale after a reorder until a full save;
    - can't take anything out, so removals, changed hybrid forms and
      signed-file appends each special-case it (`Mode`, :47–59), and a
      saved OCR layer can't be cleared at all;
    - on any page tree that isn't the identity, rewrites every page dict
      into one flat `/Pages` node, so a crop or a reorder of 7,000 pages
      writes 7,000 dicts, and drops `/PageLabels`;
    - rewrites every mark of a dirty page as a new object on every save.

    [Slices 9, 10 and 18.]
15. **The outline is rebuilt on every rewrite** by `write_outline`
    (save.rs:1318), which keeps only Title, Dest, C, F and links: `/A`
    actions, `/SE`, named destinations and the closed state are lost.
    Labels the model didn't read are dropped on a reorder. [Slices 8 and
    9.]
16. **A derived source costs a file, a thread and a full rewrite per
    edit.**
    - Each crop, watermark or strip writes a whole PDF per source
      (`derived_copy`, save.rs:727; `cropped_copy`, :714) to the
      plaintext cache (`generated::store`, called at sheaf-ops
      derive.rs:71, :154 and crop.rs:150), form values included, and
      never deletes it.
    - Each opens its own PDFium thread (`RenderHandle::open`,
      sheaf-render lib.rs:1063), never freed.
    - The plans are applied to whatever state is current when they
      finish (page_insert.rs:319–326, :450–456, :562–569), with
      `NoProgress` and no cancel. Two overlapping crops can undo each
      other (inferred).

    [Slices 15, 16, 17 and 19.]
17. **Watermarks.**
    - One new ExtGState and one new font or image per page
      (watermark.rs:242, :250, :256): an image watermark on 7,000 pages
      embeds 7,000 copies.
    - Text is written as UTF-8 into a WinAnsi font
      (watermark.rs:182–196), so "é" shows as "Ã©".
    - The settings, including a local image path, go into the file as
      `/SheafSettings` (watermark.rs:174, :216).

    [Slices 6 and 16.]
18. **Removing headers and watermarks is lossy.** `stamps::strip`
    (stamps.rs:457):
    - round-trips through lopdf's `Content` (f32 numbers, inline
      images);
    - skips a stream it can't decode without a word (:478), so a header
      can end up drawn twice (inferred);
    - works one stream at a time, so it misses a marked range that spans
      two streams;
    - replaces the stream object in place (:485), so every page that
      shares the stream changes (blank pages share one).

    `has_artifacts` (stamps.rs:495) decompresses every page's content to
    look. [Slices 6 and 16.]
19. **Mark appearances and stamps.**
    - `content_literal` (annot.rs:1515) writes everything outside ASCII
      and U+00A0–U+00FF as `?`, curly quotes, dashes and € included.
      `wrap_lines` (:1535) uses an average width.
    - Each mark gets its own inline fonts (`font_resources`, :1561) and
      ExtGState.
    - `to_pdf_in` (annot.rs:1120) re-imports page stamps from cache
      stems on every save, with a labeled box when the cache file is
      gone, and re-embeds attachments from the cache on every write.
    - Stamp placement ignores the form's BBox origin and the stamp
      page's `/Rotate` (inferred).

    [Slice 6.]
20. **Five coordinate conventions.**
    - Marks and OCR words are in source-displayed space
      (`annot::PageGeom`); widgets are in user space before `/Rotate`
      (forms/field.rs); bookmark XYZ is in the displayed frame and isn't
      shifted by a crop (inferred); header/footer and watermark layout
      is in the shown frame at write time; tapes have their own turned
      frame.
    - There are two geometry readers, `save::geom_of` and
      `file::page_geom`.
    - WIT's `page-info` width and height include sheaf's rotation, while
      its coordinates don't.

    [Slice 5.]
21. **Exports are a separate pipeline.**
    - `subset` (save.rs:86) fakes a `Snapshot` and keeps every field.
      Its outline rule (a parent becomes a heading) differs from
      DeletePages' (children are promoted), and an unsaved Bates header
      is renumbered per subset.
    - `extract_pages` (save.rs:72) and split don't refuse the open file,
      though Clean Copy (:34) and Export Flattened (:182) do.
    - `flatten_annots` (save.rs:200) ignores the Print flag, merges the
      page's content into one new stream, keeps widgets live, falls back
      to the first `/AP /N` state (`normal_appearance`, :354; M10 bug 10)
      and doesn't prune structure references.
    - Clean Copy keeps `/SheafHeaderFooter` and `SheafSettings`.

    [Slice 19.]
22. **Document content in temp and cache files.**
    - The layers panel writes a decrypted temp copy of the primary
      (layers_panel.rs:84).
    - Recovery writes `sheaf-recovered-<pid>-<ms>.pdf` and never deletes
      it (docview.rs:1024).
    - Print and export write temp files (docview.rs:3505; sheaf-ops
      export.rs:113).
    - Tape and stamp pictures go to the cache, and the generated cache
      (generated.rs) is never cleaned.

    [Slices 6, 9, 13, 14 and 19.]
23. **Recovery is limited to what the incremental writer can express.**
    The sidecar is `incremental_section` (incremental.rs:39), so a
    document with an unsaved header/footer or an encrypted file gets
    none. It's checked only by the file's length (recovery.rs:58), and
    every autosave re-parses the whole primary. [Slices 9 and 10.]
24. **Rendering.**
    - Hiding is engine state, replayed on every open (docview.rs:298,
      :1722, :1901; layers_panel.rs:110; comments_panel.rs:621), each
      time followed by a global invalidate. Print preview, OCR and
      plugins share those handles, so they inherit the view's filters
      (inferred).
    - Caches keyed by `(source, index)` miss a content edit that keeps
      the source, and every save drops all tiles.
    - The unsaved header/footer is a GPUI overlay (docview.rs:5196) that
      isn't drawn under a view rotation, and it computes every page's
      Bates numbers for each page it draws (:5210).
    - Find and Patterns search only the primary engine plus unsaved OCR
      layers (`index_of_source_page`, docview.rs:350; M11 bug 6).

    [Slices 13, 14 and 17.]
25. **Ids are guessed after apply, and drags overwrite.**
    - The new mark is "the last mark on the page" (docview.rs:1965,
      :2476); paste uses `skip(before)` (markup_tools.rs:1684); adoption
      finds the mark by `/NM` (adopt.rs:87); a new bookmark is the next
      one in pre-order (outline_panel.rs:206), which is wrong when the
      selected bookmark has children (inferred); combine finds its pages
      after the old count.
    - sheaf-ops `combine` applies one `AnnotAdd` version per mark
      (combine.rs:175).
    - Drags write back whole-record clones taken at mouse-down, so a
      plugin or OCR change made during the drag is lost (inferred).

    [Slices 11 and 12.]
26. **Three apply funnels, and a relayout per edit.** `finish_edit`
    (docview.rs:1259); hand-written `touched()` and `after_model_change`
    pairs in `insert_file` (docview.rs:2643), page_insert.rs and
    blank_review.rs; and OCR's `apply_in_job`. `touched()`
    (docview.rs:419) clears every pane's layout on every edit, undo or
    jump. [Slice 12.]
27. **Plugins.**
    - No version check anywhere: a batch is applied to the document as
      it is when the call returns, up to 30 s later. Batches apply only
      on `Ok`, each as its own version, and are dropped otherwise.
    - `edits.submit` returns nothing (wit/sheaf.wit:407), so a batch
      can't refer to what it creates.
    - `sign_marks` (plugins.rs:1039) fills `<<name>>`, `<<initials>>`
      and an empty author with the user's identity, which the plugin can
      then read back. `keep_unseen` (plugins.rs:1066) repairs
      whole-record updates from the current mark.
    - `annotations()` returns only sheaf marks, with author names, and
      `annot-delete` reaches only sheaf marks (wit/sheaf.wit:254).
    - amount-crossref and duplicate-pages keep session PageIds in
      `PENDING` statics keyed by path
      (plugins/amount-crossref/src/lib.rs:120,
      plugins/duplicate-pages/src/lib.rs:168), so after a reopen they
      can act on the wrong pages (inferred).
    - Only the plugin that made an edit is skipped for its event, so two
      plugins can ping-pong (inferred).

    [Slices 11 and 20.]
28. **Hot spots at 7,000 pages.** `index_of` (model.rs:268) is linear
    inside AnnotAdd, Insert and CommentsDelete; `page_positions` (:302)
    builds a HashMap per call; `fresh_annot_id` (:2801) scans every mark
    through `find_annot` (:217); crop looks up each id linearly; the
    header/footer's `covers()` is `Vec::contains`; `has_artifacts`
    decompresses every page; `strip_sheaf_annots` parses every
    `/SheafAnnot` on every rewrite. [Slices 8, 15, 16 and 17.]
29. **What M10 slices 3–5 left for this** (the worker's notes, also at
    the end of `m10.md`).
    - Derived copies route the page object, `/Annots`, fields and
      structure parents back to the original at save time
      (`SourceInfo::derived`).
    - `Field::copy` and `Field::under` stand in for "which instance of
      which file's form", and both writers key their copy maps by
      (source, copy).
    - `rebase_to_saved` only resets fields to the new primary (copy 0,
      no parent); their objects in a rewritten file are stale.
    - Two writers carry the same page logic, derived pages, own objects
      for copies, the form merge and the form rules.
    - Notices travel as `EditOutcome` fields (`forms_renamed`,
      `signatures_in`) that each caller reads, and Combine's can't reach
      the window it opens.

    [Slices 8, 9, 11, 17 and 19.]
30. **The docs have drifted.** `docs/document-model.md` describes a
    linear `Vec` history with a `usize` cursor (:41–42), edits as pure
    functions (:46), `Arc<Vec<PageRef>>` (:80), and says lopdf doesn't
    write incremental updates. [Every slice updates its section; slice
    20 makes the final pass.]

## Slices

**Rules for every slice:**
- It is one commit on the M10.5 branch, cut from `main`, and the app
  works at that commit.
- It deletes something, and adds its retired names to
  `tests/retired.rs`.
- It updates its own section of `docs/document-model.md`.
- Before the slice's list is final, a `cargo check` on a branch confirms
  that nothing live still calls a symbol it deletes.
- It extends the 7k gate harness where it touches a gated path.

**Sizes:** S = 1, M = 2, L = 3, XL = 5. **About 68 points over 20
slices.**

| # | Slice | Size | Headline deletion |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | History stores what changed; the retired ledger | L | `Ids`, seeds, `replay_step`, deep-equality no-op, in-place amend |
| 2 | Spikes, heavy fixtures and the gate harness | M | the spike code (verdicts and harness stay) |
| 3 | Lazy sources and the Store | L | `SourceInfo`'s bytes, sizes and password, `set_source_sizes`, the layout size walk |
| 4 | One open, gated | M | `set_initial_*`, `opened_state_mut`, six late loaders, `sheaf_ops::open` |
| 5 | PageSpace and absolute rotation | L | `Rebase.shift`, `TextLayer::shifted`, `annot::PageGeom`, the rotation delta |
| 6 | Content toolkit and fonts | L | four encoders, `has_artifacts`, `strip_ops`' round trip, cache-stem stamps, `recover_cached` |
| 7 | The serializer | L | lopdf `save_to` in `finish`, the `/Encrypt` strip, `write_atomic` without fsync |
| 8 | Records | XL | `removed_comments`, `Doc.foreign_annots`, `Document.forms`, linear scans, slice 1's keys |
| 9 | Rewrite from records; saves are outputs | XL | `build_rewrite`, incremental.rs, `rebase_to_saved`, the projection, dirty flags, `Signed` and its Append offer, the length-only disk check |
| 10 | Append, the planner and the removal check | XL | the always-Rewrite rule, Full-only recovery, slice 9's Rewrite-or-Cancel ask for signed files |
| 11 | Ops and Tx | XL | `Edit`, `compute`, `EditOutcome`, `SourceId(u32)` registrations, id guessing, `form_notice`, `insert_runs` |
| 12 | One funnel and the history budget | XL | three app funnels, the job API, `reveal_new_bookmarks`, per-edit relayout, pinned saved versions |
| 13 | Realizer, pool, render keys, view inputs | XL | per-source threads, hide replays, layers temp file, GPUI header overlay |
| 14 | Text service; print and images from bytes | M | `CharsKey`, `layer_hits`, `merge_source_hits`, print/export temp files |
| 15 | Crop as geometry | S | `cropped_copy`, crop's derived file |
| 16 | Watermark, background and strip | L | `watermark::install/read`, `SheafSettings`, derive::watermark |
| 17 | Header/footer and Bates; derived sources gone | L | `stamps::install` and `strip`, `Derived`, `derived_copy`, `copy_form`, derive.rs |
| 18 | OCR as content | M | `text_layer::install/strip`, the legacy OCR shape, plugin OCR overrides |
| 19 | Exports, memory sources, Session, `sheaf edit` | XL | `subset`, `print_pdf`, `flatten_annots`, `export_*`, generated.rs, write-then-reopen Combine |
| 20 | Plugin API 0.4 | L | WIT 0.2/0.3, `World`, `Batch`, fixture wasm, the 0.3 adapter |

**Why this order:**
- **Exact replay and the ledger come first (1),** since every later
  slice relies on them.
- **Measured risks come second (2),** as their own commit with written
  criteria, so the group size, the caps and the box-edit path are
  decided by numbers before the records slice.
- **The Store comes before anything stores handles (3).**
- **Open (4), PageSpace (5) and the content toolkit (6) run on today's
  DocState,** so records (8) and ops (11) are written once, in final
  coordinates and with the final appearance code. Op JSON never changes
  meaning.
- **The serializer (7) comes before records,** so the old writers prove
  it, encryption included, before they are deleted. It depends on
  nothing in the model.
- **Records (8), then Rewrite from records (9) at once.** Saves become
  outputs in slice 9, which deletes the projection, incremental.rs and
  `rebase_to_saved`. The projection, and `rebase_to_saved` adapted to
  records, live for one slice and cross no coordinate or appearance
  change.
- **Append (10) right after,** so saves are always Rewrites for one
  slice only. The planner needs keys and records, not ops.
- **Ops (11) against the final records and the final emitter.**
  `compute`, adapted to the new tables in slice 8, lives until here and
  writes the same records lowering will. `SourceId` becomes `BlobId`
  here, once form instances replace registrations per insert.
- **The funnel (12) after Append and ops,** so `Snapshot` is O(1) by
  then.
- **The realizer (13–14) before any content feature moves (15–18),** so
  crops and generators show on screen.
- **Consumers last (19–20).** M10 resumes after slice 20 (proposal 11).

1. **History stores what changed; the retired ledger** (L; proposals 12
   and 13; bugs 6–9).
   - `Prim::Put { key, before, after }` over today's DocState fields, as
     transitional keys: Pages, Outline, AnnotsOf(page), TextLayer(page),
     RemovedOf(page), Labels, HeaderFooter, Field(id), WidgetsOf(page).
   - `compute` writes through a recording context, and `Step` becomes
     `{ edit, line, prims }`.
   - Checked replay: a `Mismatch` shows as "History is damaged at
     version N".
   - One `IdAlloc`, never rewound.
   - A no-op is an empty prim list after coalescing per key.
   - describe runs at apply, against the pre-edit state, and is stored.
   - **VersionIds name immutable states:** `apply_in_job`'s amend
     replaces the cursor version with a new id under the same parent,
     and so does `rebase_to_saved`, which lives until slice 9.
   - `snapshot_bytes` and `step_bytes` replace the 256-byte guesses.
   - `tests/retired.rs`, matching qualified paths (`syn` over `crates/`,
     `vendor/` excluded).
   - **Deletes:**
     - `Ids { next_page, next_outline, next_field, seed }`, the reseed
       per step, and `replay_step` with its max-merge;
     - the replay-failed warn + `None` branches in undo, redo and jump;
     - deep DocState equality as no-op detection;
     - describe-against-parent and `VersionView`'s count fallback;
     - `marginal`'s guesses (model.rs:1798–1814);
     - the in-place amend.
   - **Checks:**
     - A proptest over all 21 `Edit` kinds, plus batches, jobs,
       branches, undo, redo and jump, and random trims at a snapshot
       keep-rate of 0: every version rebuilt from prims equals the
       original under a test-only deep `Eq`, with identical ids.
     - One corrupted before-value yields `Mismatch`.
     - The batch "move pages 3–4 before 1, then delete page 4" describes
       the right page numbers.
     - Each kind's no-op pushes nothing, and a batch x → y → x pushes
       nothing.
     - An OCR amend gives the cursor a new VersionId, and the old id no
       longer resolves.
     - A bench on 7,000 pages with 20,000 marks: AnnotAdd under 1 ms,
       and a full reorder under 2 ms.
     - The ledger test passes.
2. **Spikes, heavy fixtures and the gate harness** (M; proposal 16).
   - Its own commit; the spike code lives under `bench/`, never in
     `crates/`.
   - **Spike A, encrypted overlay sections.** RC4-128, AES-128 and
     AES-256 bases, sections encrypted through `encrypt_object`, loaded
     through the two-segment `FPDF_LoadCustomDocument`.
   - **Spike B, overlays on real files.** It covers:
     - the 402-file pool sample;
     - corpus forms with widgets, after a page is replaced in place;
     - xref-stream, hybrid, linearized, damaged and leading-junk bases.

     It measures open, warm and cold 1024 px renders, and RSS per
     engine.
   - **Spike C, render-time box edits.** A crop inside the base's
     visible box, through a matrix and clip on the base engine.
   - **Written criteria:**
     - A passes if all three render pixel-equal to a decrypted Full
       write.
     - B passes per base class if at least 99% of sampled pages are
       pixel-equal (appearances only) to a Full write, and RSS per
       engine stays within the cap it sets. A failing class realizes as
       a standalone PDF per group.
     - C passes if it is pixel-equal to a written crop on 100% of the
       sample. If it fails, crops realize as overlays.
   - **Verdicts.** GROUP, the LRU count, the engine byte cap, the
     background budget and the thumbnail fill rate go into
     `docs/document-model.md` before slice 8.
   - **Fixture generators** (`bench/gen.py`):
     - the **heavy 7k**: embedded subset fonts per section, an xref
       stream with object streams, about 20k annotations including
       widgets, 2k bookmarks, labels;
     - a **scanned 7k** of about 500 MB.
   - **`tests/gate_7k.rs`** (ignored) records today's baselines on both
     fixtures and the synthetic one.
   - **The planning measurements' scripts** are committed under
     `bench/`: `gen.py` (the synthetic file, which grows the heavy and
     scanned generators), and `bench.py`, `real.py`, `warm.py` and
     `form.py` (PDFium through ctypes), so proposal 16's numbers and the
     baselines can be re-run.
   - **Deletes:** the spike code, at the end of the slice. The verdicts,
     fixtures, scripts and harness stay.
   - **Checks:** the harness runs on all three fixtures and prints
     baselines, and the verdict section is present with every number
     filled in.
3. **Lazy sources and the Store** (L; bugs 4 and 9).
   - **LazyPdf** in vendored lopdf:
     - the xref and trailer at open, `get_object` on demand, the ObjStm
       cache;
     - per-object decryption, stream-dict strings included, and `/EFF`;
     - the header offset in `XrefFacts`;
     - one `Limits`;
     - an eager fallback with `base_sound = false`;
     - a positional-read byte source.
   - `Object::Real` becomes `f64`.
   - `Store`, `Blob`, `BlobRef` and `Src`, with byte-compared interning;
     `Source` holds its `Blob`.
   - **`SourceInfo` shrinks to a registration.** Its bytes, page sizes
     and password move into the `Blob` and `Src`, and a registration
     keeps only `{ src: Src, name, derived }`.
     - `SourceId(u32)` stays as the registration handle until slice 11.
       Today's form identity is per registration: every insert registers
       anew, and `take_in_fields`' join applies only within one
       registration's runs. Switching to `SourceId = BlobId` before form
       instances exist would merge two inserts of the same bytes into
       one form.
     - Derived copies and generated-cache files become interned blobs. A
       derived copy's registration keeps its `derived` record.
   - **Facts:** the page table with inherited attributes and `Geom`,
     repeated page objects, per-page `/Annots` lists, conformance.
   - `forms::read` and the layout sizes read `LazyPdf`.
   - `HistoryStats.blob_bytes`, and a real `undone_source_bytes`.
   - **Deletes:**
     - `SourceInfo`'s `data`, `bytes`, `page_sizes`, `page_count`,
       `first_page` and `password` (moved into `Blob` and `Src`), and
       `set_source_sizes`;
     - the PDFium size walk for layout;
     - the decrypt-and-strip in the read-only paths that moved;
     - the hard-coded `undone_source_bytes`.
   - **Checks:**
     - Every corpus object read lazily equals lopdf's eager load
       (skipped without the corpus).
     - The RC4-128, AES-128 and AES-256 fixtures decrypt per object
       identically.
     - An attachment-only `/EFF` fixture decrypts its attachment.
     - A file with a 100-byte prefix reads identically.
     - Fuzz targets cover the lazy reader and the page tree.
     - Cyclic `/Kids`, a `/Parent` loop, a 10,000-deep chain, an ObjStm
       bomb and a 4,096-section chain each give typed errors within the
       caps.
     - `Geom`'s displayed sizes equal PDFium's on every corpus page.
     - The geometry walk takes under 100 ms on the synthetic 7k and
       under 250 ms on the heavy one.
     - A forced blob collision is refused.
     - Inserting the same file twice still gives two registrations and
       two forms.
     - The save suites pass with f64.
     - Inserting a 400-page scan, undoing and pruning frees its bytes
       (the Store's counter drops).
4. **One open, gated** (M; proposal 28; bug 10).
   - `open::{start, finish}` builds today's DocState shape: geometry,
     sheaf marks, fields, the outline with `/C`, `/F` and closed state,
     labels, and the header/footer. It never lexes content.
   - The Loading state:
     - edit and plugin commands are grayed out;
     - `commit` returns `NotReady`;
     - plugin calls are queued until ready, and `opened` fires after.
   - The CLI and headless code use the same open.
   - **Deletes:**
     - `set_initial_outline`, `_outline_styles`, `_labels`,
       `_header_footer`, `_annots` and `_fields`;
     - `opened_state_mut`;
     - the six "arrived after edits; ignored" loaders;
     - `load_sheaf_annots`' async path;
     - `sheaf_ops::open`.
   - **Checks:**
     - Over the corpus, the root's facts equal the old loaders' (counts
       per manifest row).
     - App and headless opens produce equal states.
     - First paint is unchanged.
     - **Ready within 1 s on the heavy 7k.** If that's missed, edits
       wait with progress, and no late facts are added.
     - An edit before ready returns `NotReady`.
     - A plugin's `opened` never comes before the facts (a slow-loader
       race test).
     - A queued plugin call runs after ready.
     - A grep finds no "arrived after edits".
5. **PageSpace and absolute rotation** (L; proposal 21; bugs 12 and
   20).
   - **`Geom::{visible, shown, to_view, from_view}`** is the only
     conversion. Marks, OCR words, bookmark `DestView`s, regions and
     connectors are stored in PageSpace, on today's DocState. The plugin
     0.3 adapter converts at the edge.
   - **`/SheafAnnot` v2,** with the canonical staleness digest and tick
     ids, is written by the old writers:
     - v1 is converted from the standard keys;
     - duplicate `sheaf:<id>` names get fresh ids at open;
     - stale marks open as others' comments, in today's list.
   - **Rotation** is absolute in today's `model::PageRef` struct, and
     render jobs get the delta.
   - **Derived crops** keep user space, so `RebasePages`' shift is zero.
   - **The clipboard** holds view-frame geometry, and paste converts
     through the target's frame.
   - **`geom_of` and `page_geom`** become thin wrappers returning
     `Geom`, kept for the old writers until slice 9.
   - **Deletes:**
     - `Rebase.shift` and its shifting of marks and OCR;
     - `TextLayer::shifted`;
     - `annot::PageGeom`;
     - the outline frame conversions in the writers;
     - the app's `rotate_pt`/`unrotate_pt` conventions;
     - `model::PageRef`'s rotation delta and its reset in
       `rebase_to_saved`;
     - `/SheafTick<id>`.
   - **Checks:**
     - A property test over `/Rotate` × view rotation × CropBox offsets
       × MediaBox off the origin: `to_view ∘ from_view` is the identity
       within 1/16 pt.
     - Every mark kind round-trips on `/Rotate` 0, 90, 180 and 270 with
       offset boxes.
     - A highlight that is cropped, rotated, saved and reopened still
       covers the same glyphs.
     - A bookmark XYZ on a cropped page lands on the same content.
     - A v1 fixture's rects equal v2's within 0.01 pt.
     - A stale mark opens as another tool's comment and isn't reverted.
     - A mark re-serialized by another tool (reals reformatted,
       `/Contents` as UTF-16BE, `/Rect` corners swapped) stays a sheaf
       mark.
     - A page duplicated by another tool opens with distinct ids and one
       notice.
     - Widget hit testing equals today's on rotated corpus pages.
     - Paste between pages with different `/Rotate` and CropBox keeps
       the visual position.
6. **The content toolkit and fonts** (L; proposals 17, 22 and 29; bugs
   17–19 and 22).
   - **`content/{lex, marked, balance, splice, names, text_bag}`:**
     inline images by PDFium's rules, ranges across `/Contents` entries,
     `Balance`, safe splices, names used, and decoded TextBags.
   - **PageBlocks:** the origins `Sheaf`, `SheafLegacy`, `Acrobat` and
     `Authored`; sheaf blocks found by stream marker and by
     marked-content marker; isolation streams; the legacy OCR pattern;
     nested-in-forms.
   - **ContentWriter,** with named property lists and `place_form`.
   - **Fonts:** the `Font` trait, Std14 AFM, the exact encoders and
     `Glyphless`. Embedded fonts are GID-preserving.
   - **Writers moved onto the toolkit:** mark appearances, OCR stream
     writing, tapes, stamps and the comment summary (still called by the
     old writers).
   - **Readers moved onto the toolkit:** `forms::check`'s `da_font`
     (forms/check.rs:760) lexes `/DA` with `content::lex`, and the stamp
     library's page-to-form (stamp_lib.rs:408) takes the page's
     `/Contents` through the toolkit's concatenation, not
     `get_page_content`.
   - **`'?'` plus a notice** for unencodable text, in marks and
     generators.
   - **Names:** page-stamp XObjects get `PageRes`-style names.
   - Empty per-crate `clippy.toml` files are added; their bans land per
     the lint ledger.
   - **Deletes:**
     - `annot::content_literal`'s encoder, `font_resources` and
       `wrap_lines`' average width;
     - `stamps::win_ansi`/`pdf_literal` (moved);
     - `stamps::strip_ops`' lopdf `Content` round trip (strip uses
       splice now);
     - `has_artifacts`' decompress scan;
     - the summary's own writer and wrap;
     - the cache-stem stamp import and its labeled-box fallback;
     - attachments re-embedded from the cache on every write;
     - `file::recover_cached`, and `stamp_preview`'s cache writes (the
       tape PDFs and the PNG pictures, stamp_preview.rs:31, :68 and
       :118–121);
     - `da_font`'s `Content::decode` and stamp_lib.rs's
       `get_page_content`;
     - `/SheafStamp` as a written XObject name.
   - **Checks:**
     - Lexing plus a no-op splice is byte-identical on every corpus
       content stream.
     - An inline image containing `EI` survives a strip.
     - An ignored differential test compares the lexer's boundaries with
       PDFium's page objects over the pool.
     - A marked range spanning two streams is removed.
     - A malformed stream is an error naming the page.
     - A drop inside BT…ET that would remove a `Tf` or `Td` is refused,
       and the pixels outside a removed artifact's bbox are unchanged.
     - Classification: Acrobat's reference files give `Acrobat`, Word-
       and LibreOffice-tagged PDFs give `Authored`, and sheaf-written
       files give `Sheaf` or `SheafLegacy`.
     - `Balance` matches a reference count.
     - `é € — “ ”` in a FreeText reads back through PDFium.
     - Widths equal the AFM.
     - Identical inputs give byte-identical streams.
     - The same text in two different subsets gives equal TextBags.
     - A stamp from a cropped, rotated page lands where PDFium shows it.
     - A library stamp made from a page with several `/Contents` streams
       draws as that page does.
     - `forms::check` finds the same `/DA` fonts on every corpus form.
     - Stamps and attachments survive a reopen and re-save with an empty
       cache directory.
     - CJK mark text draws `'?'`, keeps the exact `/Contents`, and gives
       one notice.
7. **The serializer** (L; proposals 23 and 33; bugs 2, 4 and 5).
   - `serialize/{object, xref, append, full, crypt, durable}`:
     - f64 reals, with non-finite values refused, and clamping;
     - one-subsection full xrefs with a free list, and appended runs
       with generation + 1 on deletes;
     - offsets relative to the header offset;
     - per-object encryption, stream-dict strings and `/EFF` included,
       with keyed-hash IVs;
     - `/Encrypt` and `/ID` kept;
     - unique temp names, preserved permissions, fsync of the file and
       the directory;
     - a minimal `FileStamp { len, mtime, tail hash }`, captured at open
       and after each save on the primary's registration
       (`SourceInfo`), and its re-check before the rename or append.
       A mismatch returns `SaveStop::Ask(Ask::FileChanged)` through the
       old entry point, as an error the app downcasts the way it does
       `WouldBreakSignatures` today, and the app asks Save As…,
